Accept forms like: 4.5, \"4.5\", \"4.5/5.0\", \"4.5 / 5.0\"\n        m = re.search(r'(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)', s)\n        score = m.group(1) if m else s\n    return f'{score}점 (5.0점 만점)'\n\n\ndef append_followup_sentence(value) -> str:\n    base = '' if value is None else str(value)\n    suffix = '후속 심화반 검토 요망.'\n    if base.endswith(suffix):\n        return base\n    if base and not base.endswith(' '):\n        base = base + ' '\n    return base + suffix\n\n\ndef build_replacements(data: dict) -> dict:\n    repl = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        repl[k] = '' if v is None else str(v)\n\n    # Apply transformation rules\n    if '참석자수' in repl:\n        repl['참석자수'] = digits_only(data.get('참석자수'))\n    if '만족도' in repl:\n        repl['만족도'] = normalize_satisfaction(data.get('만족도'))\n    if '종합의견' in repl:\n        repl['종합의견'] = append_followup_sentence(data.get('종합의견'))\n\n    return repl\n\n\ndef is_layout_cache_tag(tag: str) -> bool:\n    # tag might be \"{namespace}local\" or \"local\"\n    lower = tag.lower()\n    return ('lineseg' in lower) or ('layoutcache' in lower)\n\n\ndef remove_layout_caches_in_paragraph(paragraph_elem: ET.Element) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Remove layout cache elements under this paragraph. Returns True if any removed.\"\"\"\n    removed = False\n\n    def recurse(parent: ET.Element):\n        nonlocal removed\n        # Iterate over a snapshot because we may remove children.\n        for child in list(parent):\n            if is_layout_cache_tag(child.tag):\n                parent.remove(child)\n                removed = True\n            else:\n                recurse(child)\n\n    recurse(paragraph_elem)\n    return removed\n\n\ndef paragraph_text_elements(paragraph_elem: ET.Element):\n    \"\"\"Return list of text elements (any namespace) that look like text nodes.\"\"\"\n    # In HWPML HWPX, text is in <hp:t>\n    text_elems = []\n    for elem in paragraph_elem.iter():\n        if elem.tag.endswith('}t') or elem.tag == 't':\n            text_elems.append(elem)\n    return text_elems\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_paragraph(paragraph_elem: ET.Element, replacements: dict) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders within a paragraph, even if split across multiple <t> runs.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Collect all <t> elements in order within paragraph\n    - Concatenate their .text ('' if None)\n    - Replace placeholders in the concatenated string\n    - If changed, write full new string into first <t>, clear rest\n\n    Returns True if text was modified.\n    \"\"\"\n    t_elems = paragraph_text_elements(paragraph_elem)\n    if not t_elems:\n        return False\n\n    texts = [(e.text or '') for e in t_elems]\n    joined = ''.join(texts)\n\n    def repl_fn(match: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = match.group(1)\n        if key in replacements:\n            return replacements[key]\n        # If missing, leave as-is to make it detectable in verification.\n        return match.group(0)\n\n    replaced = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl_fn, joined)\n    if replaced == joined:\n        return False\n\n    t_elems[0].text = replaced\n    for e in t_elems[1:]:\n        e.text = ''\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef register_namespaces_from_file(xml_path: Path):\n    for event, elem in ET.iterparse(str(xml_path), events=('start-ns',)):\n        prefix, uri = elem\n        # Avoid ValueError for duplicate registrations\n        try:\n            ET.register_namespace(prefix if prefix is not None else '', uri)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: Path, replacements: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file. Returns stats.\"\"\"\n    register_namespaces_from_file(xml_path)\n\n    tree = ET.parse(str(xml_path))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paragraphs = 0\n    removed_cache_paragraphs = 0\n\n    #
